## 4. IDENTITY — byte-compatible reimplementation recipe

Compute in this order (server/workshop.js `identifyPack` 825-987; digest at the end by `modManifestDigest`):
1. `manifest = []`, `seen = Set`; `add(rel, hash)` = first-wins dedupe by path (828-830).
2. `addText('pack.json', canonicalJson(<normalized manifest>))` — 834. **The file's BYTES are NOT hashed**; the normalized object is, and it includes the derived-free declared `layer`/`combat`/`api`/`game` plus `hasAssets` (which comes from `fs.existsSync(packDir+'/assets')`, so an empty `assets/` dir changes the hash).
3. For each `[file, records]` of `pack.files` (only the `content[]`-declared files the loader successfully parsed): `addText('<file>.json', canonicalJson(records))` — 836. Same — normalized records, not disk bytes.
4. For each `rel` of `listPackFiles(packDir)` (server/packFiles.js:42) not already `seen`: `addBytes(rel, fs.readFileSync(...))` — 846-851. This is the **whole pack directory**, recursively, every regular file, sorted by code-unit path, symlinks neither followed nor listed, no name/dir exclusions (so `README.md`, `kits/**`, `assets/**`, `.bak` all count). Unreadable → skipped silently (850).
5. `kits` count (852-863): `fs.readdirSync(packDir+'/kits').sort()`, only names ending `.js`, only `statSync().isFile()`, only those NOT starting with `_` — used only to derive `layer`/`combat`, the bytes already came from step 4.
6/6b/7/8 (864-984): extra `addBytes` passes for declared paths **already covered by step 4**, each guarded by `if (manifest.some(m => m.path === rel)) continue` and an `abs.startsWith(packDir + path.sep)` containment check — `client.panels[].module` (868-878), `client.panels[].styles[]` (882-890), `server.meta.module` (895-902), `server.battle.module` (906-913), `server.modules[].entry` (917-931), `server.room.module` (924-937), `i18n` values + `notices` values (`declaredTextFiles` 972-984). **For a data-only pack these are all no-ops** because step 4 already hashed the files; they exist for packs whose declared path lives outside the directory listing (impossible) or for historical reasons.
   - **Step 6 (the synthetic row, 959-967):** `if (typeof assetsDigest === 'string' && assetsDigest) add(ASSETS_DIGEST_PATH, assetsDigest)` where `ASSETS_DIGEST_PATH = 'assets.container.sha256'` (server/packFiles.js:28) and `assetsDigest` is the sha256 of the CONTAINER bytes, verified against the `<container>.sha256` sidecar at load (`assetsIssues` 342-422, `readSha256Sidecar` 312). It has NO bytes on disk; the download route serves the 64-char digest text itself (`containerDigestBytes` packFiles.js:77). If a real file named `assets.container.sha256` exists, step 4 already won the `seen` dedupe and this row is skipped.
9. `manifest.sort((a,b) => a.path < b.path ? -1 : a.path > b.path ? 1 : 0)` (985) — UTF-16 code-unit order, the same comparator as `packFiles.byPath` and `modCatalog.byPath`.
10. `hash = modManifestDigest(manifest)` (986) = `sha256Hex(canonicalJson(sorted [[path,hash],…]))`.

`canonicalJson(value)` (shared/modIdentity.js:168-174): scalars → `JSON.stringify(value) ?? 'null'`; arrays → `[a,b,c]` joined with `,` no spaces; objects → `{` + keys filtered for `rec[k] !== undefined` + `.sort()` (default lexicographic on UTF-16) + `"key":value` joined with `,` + `}`. No whitespace anywhere; key order irrelevant.
`modManifestDigest(manifest)` (182-188): filter entries with string `path` and string `hash` → `[path, hash]` pairs → sort by path (same comparator) → `sha256Hex(canonicalJson(pairs))`. So the pairs become `[["a.js","deadbeef…"],["b.js","…"]]`.
`sha256Hex` (122-160): hand-written sha256 over UTF-8 bytes (hand-rolled `utf8Bytes` 102-115 to avoid TextEncoder) or over a `Uint8Array`, lowercase hex, `padStart(8,'0')` per word. Node's `createHash('sha256')` produces the same string (modCatalog.js:226-228 relies on that).
Subtleties to get right: (a) step 2/3 hash canonical JSON, step 4 hashes raw bytes — mixing them up changes every hash; (b) `undefined` values vanish from canonical objects but a *present* key with value `null` does not — and `normalizePackManifest` writes `api: null`, `layer: null`, `combat: null` explicitly (1947-1950) while omitting `assets`/`client`/`server`/`routes`/`i18n`/`notices`/`forms`/`fx`/`skins` when undeclared (1817-1826, 1961, 1967) — that asymmetry is exactly what makes old packs' hashes stable; (c) `content`/`overrides`/`support`/`directToHand`/operator key order are all sorted before hashing; (d) path strings use `/` separators with no leading `./` (packFiles.js:60).
For reference, modSet digest (the room gate) = `modDigest(list)` (196-202) over sorted `[id, hash]` pairs → 64-hex; `modSetOf` (205-210) returns `{digest, packs}` sorted by id, or `null` for an empty set.

## 5. LOADER DATA PATH

`server/data.js:loadData` (67-109) is the entry: read `data/*.json` (79-86) → warn about missing expected files (87-88) → **if `workshopDir !== null`** (96): `loadWorkshop(workshopDir, {log})` (97) → optional `excludePacks` filter (98) → `applyWorkshop(out, packs)` (100) → copy merged top-level keys back into `out` (101) → `log.info?.('[workshop] applied N pack(s): ' + workshopSummary(report))` (102) → one `log.warn` per `report.errors` entry as `[workshop] <pack>: <reason>` (103) → one per `report.looks` (105) → `return deepFreeze(out)` (108). Because the overlay runs **before** `deepFreeze`, every consumer sees one ordinary frozen merged object.

`server/workshop.js:loadWorkshop(dir = WORKSHOP_DIR, {log, c2s})` 87-223, in order:
1. `readdirSync(dir, {withFileTypes:true})`, keep directories not starting with `.`, names `.sort()` (94-97). A throw (no `workshop/`) → `{dir, present:false, packs:[], errors:[]}` — **the normal plain-install case** (98-101).
2. Per pack dir: read+parse `pack.json` (105-110). `ENOENT` → `pack.json is missing`; else `pack.json is unreadable: <msg>`. **`continue` = the pack is skipped.**
3. `hasAssets = fs.existsSync(packDir + '/assets')` (112) → `normalizePackManifest(rawManifest, name, {hasAssets})` (113-117).
4. For each `file` of `manifest.pack.content`: read+parse `<file>.json`, `normalizeContentFile(file, json)` → `files[file] = content.records` (120-137). A per-file failure pushes an error and `continue`s the FILE loop (the rest of the pack still loads).
5. "Does this pack exist at all?" gate (151-163): loaded iff any of `files`, `voices`, `voiceLangs`, `bondIcons`, `itemIcons`, `art`, `skins.length`, `operators`, or any of `assets`/`client`/`server`/`routes`/`i18n`/`notices` is non-empty.
6. `playtestUnknownIds(...)` membership check → `PLAYTEST_UNKNOWN_CHESS` and **whole-pack skip** (166-173).
7. Declared-payload gates, each returning a code+reason; the FIRST issue anywhere refuses the WHOLE pack (178-209): `panelModuleIssues` (178), `assetsIssues` (189), `preDispatchIssues` (190), `i18nIssues` (193), `metaIssues` (196), `battleIssues` (198), `serverModuleIssues` (200), `roomIssues` (202), `noticesIssues` (204); combined at 205, first code+reason logged at 207.
8. Success → `packs.push({ ...manifest.pack, dir: packDir, files, ...identifyPack(packDir, manifest.pack, files, { assetsDigest: assetIssues.digest }), ...(assetIssues.digest ? { assetsDigest } : {}) })` (214-218). Note the spread order: `identifyPack`'s **derived** `layer`/`combat`/`api`/`game`/`hash`/`manifest` overwrite the declared ones on the entry.
9. After the loop: every collected error is logged as `[workshop] <pack>: <reason>` (221). Broken packs never throw; they are simply absent from `packs` and present in `errors`.

Ordering: `loadWorkshop` sorts directory NAMES (`String` default sort) but `applyWorkshop` re-sorts by `byPackId` (2661) — so the merge order is pack-id code-unit ascending, independent of `readdir` order. There is no layer A/B/C ordering anywhere.

`server/http/workshop.js:buildWorkshopDataFiles(data, workshop)` 76-85 builds the merged `/data/*.json` bodies for exactly `workshopTouchedFiles(workshop)` (server/workshop.js:999-1041): the union of `Object.keys(p.files)` with `units` remapped to `backups` (1006), plus `assets` for voices/voiceLangs/bondIcons/itemIcons/art/skins/forms/fx (1012-1033), plus `support` (1035) and `backups` for operators (1038). That map is what `static.js:255-265` serves ahead of the on-disk static mount, with `Cache-Control: no-cache` and an `ETag: "ws-<sha1(body)[:20]>"` — the ETag is a cache validator only, not an integrity gate.

## 6. COUPLING — what the DATA path needs, and where it touches CODE machinery

Needed, cannot cut: `shared/workshop.js`, `shared/modIdentity.js`, `server/packFiles.js`, `server/workshop.js:loadWorkshop` + `identifyPack` + `assetsIssues`(+`sha256FileSync`/`readSha256Sidecar`) if you keep the `assets` field, `server/data.js:loadData`, and `server/http/workshop.js:buildWorkshopDataFiles`+`workshopTouchedFiles` if browsers get merged data.
Transitive import edges of `shared/workshop.js` (171-182) — these come along whether you use the relevant fields or not: `shared/constants.js` (VOICE_SLOTS/VOICE_LANGS/DEFAULT_VOICE_LANG/MOD_API_VERSION), `shared/i18nPacks.js` (SOURCE_LANG/isLangCode), `shared/support.js` (isSupportTier), `shared/packs.js` (isVersionRange/appVersionMatches), `shared/modIdentity.js` (MOD_LAYERS), `shared/diy.js` (`requiredUnitForms`, used only by `workshopOperatorEntries` 3563), `shared/packForms.js` (FORM_TABLES/FORM_KIND_RE/FORM_SPEC_KEYS/checkFormRoles), `shared/formNames.js` (`firstPartyFormNames`), and — importantly — **`shared/protocol.js`** for `C2S` (182, used at 423 `PRE_DISPATCH_INTERCEPTS = Object.keys(C2S).sort()`). `shared/protocol.js` is 56KB and is excluded from this repo's typecheck slice (noted in modIdentity.js header 3-6). For a data-only port you can delete the `C2S` import and `PRE_DISPATCH_INTERCEPTS` together with the `server` field.
Explicitly answer to the questions asked:
- **Does `identifyPack` hash kit files?** YES, indirectly and unconditionally: step 4 hashes every regular file under the pack dir including `kits/**` and any helper. It also *counts* top-level non-`_` `kits/*.js` (854-862) purely to derive `layer`/`combat` (952/958). Consequence: you cannot drop `kits/` support from the data path and keep hashes byte-compatible — but you also never need to *execute* a kit to hash it, and you do not need `shared/kitImports.js` for identity.
- **Does `normalizePackManifest` validate `server.*`?** YES — it parses and shape-validates all five `server` members (780-983) and even enforces `combat: true` for meta/battle/matchClass (1918-1930). If you drop the `server` key from `PACK_FIELDS`, every `server`-declaring pack starts failing `PACK_UNKNOWN_FIELD` — that is a deliberate, visible behaviour change. A softer option is to keep parsing it for shape (so hashes stay identical) while never loading the modules.
- **Does the loader need `kitImports.js` to compute identity?** NO. `shared/kitImports.js` is imported at `server/workshop.js:28` and used only inside `relativeModuleUrl` (1055-1060, via `packRelativePath`) and `loadWorkshopKits` (1211-1223, via `kitImportDeclarations`/`kitImportIssues`/`rewriteKitImports`) — i.e. only on the executed-code path. `identifyPack` and `loadWorkshop`'s data half never call it.
- **Does the loader need `client.panels` machinery for identity?** Only for hashing declared module/style paths, which step 4 already covers; `panelModuleIssues` (240) is a disk-existence gate for the *executed* C layer.
- Other couplings worth knowing: `server/data.js` imports `./workshop.js`, which imports `shared/kitImports.js`, `../shared/protocol.js`, `./notices.js` — so keeping `server/data.js` as-is drags the whole code-loading module into the import graph even if you never call `loadWorkshopKits`. A data-only port should split `server/workshop.js` into a loader module (no `await import`) and a code-loading module.
- `shared/modSurface.js` is dead here (no runtime importer) and can be dropped.
- `server/modCatalog.js` re-runs `normalizePackManifest` on pack.json (107-116) and reads every listed file — it is data-only but is only required for the client download path.

## 7. CLIENT REQUIREMENTS FOR A LOCAL-ONLY DATA PACK

Short answer: **if the server already applies the overlay and serves the merged `/data/*.json`, the client needs NO change at all.** `public/js/data.js` (`createDataStore`, 130-…) fetches `${base}${DATA_FILES[name] || name + '.json'}` with default base `'/data/'` (161, 407) and parses JSON; there is no hash/digest check on `/data/*.json` anywhere on the client. The merged body is delivered by `server/http/static.js:255-265` with only `Cache-Control: no-cache` + `ETag: "ws-<sha1(body)[:20]>"` — an ETag is not an integrity gate and the client never compares it to a pack hash. Likewise `public/js/data.js` has no notion of packs.
What actually requires the mod store:
1. **Room-declared mod sets.** The only digest gate in the whole system is the room handshake, and it lives entirely in the client mod store: `welcome.mods` / `BattleSpec.mods` carry `{digest, packs}` (`modSetOf`, shared/modIdentity.js:205-210); `public/js/mods/align.js:planAlignment` (94-114) rebuilds the digest from LOCAL bytes and compares `held !== mods.digest` (113); `align.js:roomDataBase` (47-51) switches the simulation data face to `/room-data/<digest>/` only when the room's digest differs from the process digest. `public/js/battle/runner.js:1037-1043` (`dataBaseForBattle` → `roomDataBase(spec.mods, currentModSet())`) is the only consumer.
2. **What breaks if you delete `modCatalog.js` / `server/http/mods.js` / `public/js/mods/{sync,store,align}.js`:** the static imports break the bundle, not the data path — `public/js/battle/runner.js:76` imports `roomDataBase` from `../mods/align.js`, and `public/js/screens/room.js:32` and `:345,367` import `loadCatalog/planAlignment/alignRoom/missingPackIds/storeFor`. You must remove those imports/UI too. After that, `dataBaseForBattle` must degrade to a constant `'/data/'`, and you must also stop emitting `welcome.mods` (`server/index.js:172`, `:258`) so the client never sees a mod set it cannot verify. Server-side counterparts to drop: `createModsRoute`/`buildModCatalog`/`serveMods` (`server/index.js:232`, `:265`, `:272`), `roomAssets` (`server/index.js:183-197`, `server/roomAssets.js`), and the `/room-data/` route (`static.js:233-250`).
3. **What you MUST keep for a data-only pack even with no client mod code:** `workshopTouchedFiles` → `buildWorkshopDataFiles` → the `/data/*.json` merged branch (`static.js:255-265`). Miss it and the server has overlay data while the browser fetches the on-disk files — a silent split between server and client (`shared/workshop.js` header 5-12 and `server/http/workshop.js:67-75` both call this out). Nothing else on the client is needed: no assets container, no `/mods/*`, no `/workshop-*` routes, no kits, no panels, no `room-data`.
4. One caveat if you keep the room-digest feature without the download machinery: the digest still must be computable (`modDigest`/`modSetOf`) and the client still needs `modIdentity.js` (it is browser-safe by design: hand-rolled sha256 + `canonicalJson`, no node:crypto, no TextEncoder — modIdentity.js:78-160). `localPackHash` (sync.js:264) uses `crypto.subtle` with a pure-JS fallback (sync.js:54-63). Dropping sync.js alone while keeping `align.js` is not possible (align.js:16 imports `CATALOG_URL, localPackHash, missing, sync`).

UNVERIFIED / NOT CHECKED (no tests run, `node --test` broken in this sandbox; and `app/test/` is not shipped): I did not read `docs/WORKSHOP.md`, `docs/design/mod-layer.md`, `editor/server.mjs`, `server/http/{boot,common,files,routes,websocket}.js`, `server/index.js` beyond the grep above, or the remaining merge function bodies in `shared/workshop.js:2798-3514` and `3376-3520` (artClaims/artClaimCounts/mergeArtEntry) line by line — the line ranges and code lists cited above come from direct reads of the ranges I did open plus exhaustive greps of export/function/code-string sites.

The first subagent's report is extremely detailed and importantly **corrects/refines** my design in several ways:

1. **CRITICAL**: Forge *does* serve merged `/data/*.json`: `server/http/workshop.js:buildWorkshopDataFiles(data, workshop)` + `workshopTouchedFiles` (server/workshop.js:999-1041), served by `static.js:255-265` ahead of the on-disk static mount, with `Cache-Control: no-cache` + `ETag: ws-<sha1>`. And the subagent says: **if the server applies the overlay and serves merged `/data/*.json`, the client needs NO change at all.**

   This directly contradicts my recommendation (§0.3 / §3.1) that the client must merge packs itself. Let me re-verify: does OUR tree have that merged-data serving path? No — that's a Forge addition (`server/http/workshop.js`). Our `server/http/static.js` serves `/data/` from disk (URL_MOUNTS). So in OUR tree, the server-side overlay alone would NOT reach the browser — my analysis was right for our tree. But Forge's approach (serve merged data from memory with an ETag) is *simpler than my client-merge proposal* and worth adopting for us: it's ~1 module (`buildDataFiles` + a `workshopTouchedFiles` analogue) + a branch in our static.js before the disk mount.

   That's a better P1 design: **server materializes the merged body in memory and serves it at `/data/*.json` (no-cache + ETag)**, so the browser needs zero changes and there's no risk of divergence-by-implementation. The client-merge option is unnecessary. I should revise the doc: recommend the Forge-style "serve merged data" approach as primary (P1), and drop the "client must merge" hard requirement (keep the digest handshake for room-level alignment).

   Note the subtlety: the merged route must only serve files the packs touched, else you'd shadow every data file (and my concern about the client needing `/packs/<id>/<file>` disappears: the client never fetches pack files).

   Also the ETag is a cache validator, not integrity — so with packs, the client can't verify content. That's where the digest handshake comes in (our docs' requirement).

2. Merge details I got right (additive + overrides + closed keys) but now I have precise rules: merge order is `byPackId` (not layer), record merge is field-level recursive with `OVERRIDE_REPLACE_KEYS` wholesale-replace list, `PACK_ID_COLLISION` (smaller id wins), `OFFICIAL_ID_COLLISION`, `UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE_FIELD`, `EMPTY_PACK`, `ID_MISMATCH`, `EMPTY_CONTENT`, plus per-file normalizers.

3. `shared/workshop.js` has NO code execution at all (pure validation + merge); the code execution lives in `server/workshop.js` (`loadWorkshopKits`, `loadWorkshopHooks`, `dropUnavailablePreDispatchPacks`) and in the *declared* `client.panels`. So a data-only port can literally reuse Forge's `shared/workshop.js` (3,744 lines) trimmed, OR port the data subset. But note the coupling: `shared/workshop.js` imports `shared/protocol.js` for `C2S` (only for `preDispatch.intercepts` validation) — droppable; and imports packForms/formNames/support/diy/i18nPacks/constants/packs/modIdentity — several of which we don't have (`shared/packForms.js`, `shared/formNames.js`, `shared/diy.js`? we have diy data; `shared/support.js`?).

That's much smaller than 1,300-1,900 — more like 600-900 as I estimated.

的解法最省事，照抄即可：**把合并后的正文由服务端在内存里产出、并先于磁盘挂载地应答**（`server/http/workshop.js:buildWorkshopDataFiles` + `server/workshop.js:999 workshopTouchedFiles`，`static.js:255-265` 带 `Cache-Control: no-cache` 与 `ETag: ## 1. DATA PIPELINE

**Where data is loaded.** `server/data.js:59` `loadData(dir = DATA_DIR, { log = console, expected = DATA_FILES } = {})`:
- reads *every* `*.json` in `DATA_DIR` (`= ROOT/data`, `server/data.js:24`), key = basename (`:72` `const key = file.slice(0, -'.json'.length)`), `:74` `out[key] = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, file), 'utf8'))`.
- `DATA_FILES` (`:27-31`) = config, chess, bonds, garrisons, items, bands, effects, choices, enemies, factions, waves, stages, bosses, tokens, assets, backups, hextech.
- Errors: unreadable dir → warn `[data] cannot read … running without game data` (`:69`); bad file → `[data] skipping` (`:76`); missing expected → one warn listing them (`:80`).
- `:81` `return deepFreeze(out);` — `deepFreeze` at `:39-50` freezes every nested object iteratively. **No overlay/merge seam exists today.**

**`getData` / `resetData`.** `:92-95` `getData({dir=DATA_DIR, log=console})` → `if (!singleton) singleton = loadData(dir, {log});` — process-wide, first call wins, later calls ignore options. `:98` `resetData() { singleton = null; }` — "tests / hot reload" only. There is no `workshopDir`/`excludePacks` option (Forge adds both at its `server/data.js:67` and `:119`).

**Who calls it.** `server/index.js:29` imports getData/loadData; `:109` `const data = opts.dataDir ? loadData(dataDir, { log }) : getData({ dir: dataDir, log });` (one call at boot, then threaded into `createSessionStack`). Other callers: `server/lobby.js:1517` `this.getData()` (wrapped in try/catch → frozen `{}`), `server/sim/content/support/index.js:41` `DATA = getData({log: QUIET}) || {}`, `server/match/builtinMeta.js:321`, plus ~20 test files. **No caller ever reloads it in a running server.**

**How data reaches the browser.** `server/http/static.js`:
- `:61` mounts `{ prefix: '/data/', name: 'data', dir: path.resolve(dataDir) }`; `/data/foo.json` → `:112` `path.join(mount.dir, ...segments)` → `:157` `await serveFile(...)`.
- **The bytes are the raw files on disk. There is no per-room or per-mod filtering of `/data/*.json`.** (docs/MODS.md:77 says so explicitly: "`/data/*.json` 是静态文件、所有房间一样".)
- Caching (`server/http/files.js`): `:199` `baseTag = ${stat.size.toString(16)}-${floor(mtimeMs).toString(16)}`, `:200` strong `ETag`, `:206` `Cache-Control: cacheControlFor(...)`, `:208` `Last-Modified`, `:212` 304 via `isNotModified`. `cacheControlFor` (`:186-191`): `.html/.htm` → `no-cache`; `?v=` → `IMMUTABLE_CACHE`; only `public/` + first segment in `LONG_CACHE_DIRS = ['assets','fonts','vendor']` (`:69`) → `public, max-age=86400`; **everything else, i.e. all of `/data/`, `/shared/`, `/sim/`, is `no-cache`** (revalidate every load). JSON is gzipped (`COMPRESSIBLE` `:57-60` includes `.json`).
- Special route in the same handler: `/data.js` (`:78-84`) serves `DATA_SHIM_JS` (`:33-39`, ETag `shim-<hex len>`, `no-cache`): `export function getData() { return getSimData() || {}; }` / `export function resetData() {}` — the browser stand-in of server/data.js. Also `/data/local-assets.json` → empty manifest instead of 404 (`:142-146`).

**Is the browser copy hashed/validated?** **No.** `public/js/data.js:130` `createDataStore(opts)` fetches `base + (DATA_FILES[name] || name + '.json')` (`:169`) with `{ cache: 'no-cache' }` (`:177`); the only integrity handling is JSON-parse → `badJson` (`:182`) and transient-failure retry `RETRY_DELAYS_MS = [600, 2000]` (`:99`). Cache is per-page (`entries` Map `:139`). No ETag/If-None-Match logic client-side, no digest check, no build/version stamp on data.

**Does the sim re-derive from these files?** Yes, heavily — `server/sim/simdata.js` is the normalisation layer: `simdata.js:1-11` header ("The sim never reads raw JSON records directly: every chess / enemy / token record is normalised once into an engine 'def'"), sources in order `(1)` injected `Battle opts.data`, `(2)` Node → `server/data.js→getData()` + research JSON fallback via `server/sim/nodeData.js`, browser → whatever `setSimData(data)` injected (`DESIGN §14`). Normalizers: `flattenBlackboard:52`, `normalizeSkill:117`, `normalizeChess:173`, `normalizeEnemy:260`, `normalizeToken:309`, `normalizeStage:363`, `spawnsFromTemplate:451`, `freezeDef:544`, `class DataSource:565`, `setSimData:827`, `getSimData:835`, `reloadDataSources:840`, `toDataSource:846`. `nodeData.js:24` `mod.getData({log:QUIET})` / `:34` `mod.resetData?.()`.
**Room-scoped filtering exists only in the match layer, not in this pipeline:** `server/lobby.js:1529` `dataFor(room)` → `:1535` `filterData(base, room.mods)` (memoised by `room.mods.join(',')` at `:1532-1533`), consumed at `lobby.js:1201` (`checkDiyPicks`), `:1264`, and `startMatch` (`opts.data = this.dataFor(room)`). So: **the seam a data-layer overlay would hook is `loadData` before `deepFreeze` (`server/data.js:81`), exactly where Forge puts it** (`参考对象/…Forge…/app/server/data.js:96-107`, `applyWorkshop(out, packs)` then `deepFreeze`).

## 2. EXISTING PACK PLUMBING TO IMITATE

**`shared/packs.js` (291 lines, format; no Node builtin — header `:6-7`)** — exports: `PACK_INDEX_VERSION = 1` (`:38`), `PACK_MANIFEST = 'pack.json'` (`:40`), `PACK_INDEX_FILE = 'index.json'` (`:42`), `PACKS_URL = '/packs/'` (`:44`), `PACK_TYPES` (`:55-62`: `lang` supported/live, `assets`+`data` `status:'planned'`), `isPackId` (`:68` `/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}$/`), `isPackPath` (`:75`, relative, ≤200, no dot/hidden segment), `isVersionRange` (`:111`), `appVersionMatches` (`:123`), `normalizeManifest(raw, ctx)` (`:151` → `{manifest|null, problems[], warnings[]}`), `packIndexEntry(manifest, extra)` (`:214`), `buildPackIndex(entries,{app})` (`:241`), `readPackIndex(json, type)` (`:256`), `langMetaOf(e)` (`:274`).
Key patterns: **problems block loading, warnings do not** (`:143-144`): `:154` `'the manifest is not a JSON object'`, `:158` `'no "type"'`, `:159` `` `unknown type "${type}" (known: …)` ``, `:196` `` `no "files.${role}"` ``, `:197` `` `"files.${role}": "…" is not a path inside the pack folder` ``, `:198` `` `"files.${role}" must be a .json file` ``; warnings `:163` id mismatch, `:165` bad range, `:168` `made for app ${range}; this is ${app}`, `:201` unknown role ignored. File roles + extensions come from the type: `:58` `lang.files = { ui: {required:true, ext:['.json']}, data: {ext:['.json']} }` — **the "nothing else in a pack folder is ever served" rule is derived from this table** (`:51-52`).

**`server/packs.js` (245 lines, registry)** — `LANG_DIR = 'i18n'` (`:25`), `MAX_JSON_BYTES = 8*1024*1024` (`:27`), `readJson(abs)` (`:41`), `inside(dir, abs)` via `realpathSync` (`:53-59` — a pack file may never lead out of its folder), `fileUrl(id, rel)` (`:61`), `scanPacks({publicDir,dataDir,packsDir},{app})` (`:69`) → `{packs, skipped, warnings}`:
- `:100-139` folder packs: `packs/<id>/pack.json`; no manifest → `skipped` `'no pack.json'` (`:107`); planned type → `'type "assets" is planned (…), not loaded by this version'` (`:114-116`); each declared file must exist (`:123` `files.${role}: ${rel} is missing`) and stay inside (`:124` `leads out of the pack folder`); a missing file skips the whole pack (`:127`).
- `:141-155` one pack per id (case-folded) and per language; later duplicates `skipped` naming the holder.
- `packIndexOf(packs,{app})` (`:164`), `signature(dirs)` (`:173-191`: names+sizes+mtime of everything the scan reads, packs dir depth 3), `createPackRegistry(dirs,{log,app,recheckMs=1000})` (`:199`) returning `{ refresh(force) 226, list(type) 228, index() 230, report() 232, servable(id, rel) 238 }`. **Live pickup without restart** (`:207-222`), and **log-once** dedupe `tell()` (`:205`) so a broken pack warns once, not per request (`:219-220`).

**`shared/i18nPacks.js` (177 lines)** — `SOURCE_LANG='zh'` (`:30`), `canonicalLang` (`:38`), `isLangCode` (`:54`), `primaryLang` (`:57`), `languageName` (`:64`), `langList` (`:78`), `langFields` (`:100`), `packMeta` (`:126`), `countStrings` (`:139`), `computeChain` (`:154`), `scriptOf` (`:177`).

**`test/i18n-packs.test.js` (read tool reports 475; `Measure-Object -Line` says 456 — line-ending count difference; not a discrepancy)** — end-to-end pattern: builds a synthetic checkout in `os.tmpdir()` (`:45` `tmpdir`, `:52-70` `exampleRoot()` with broken/planned/duplicate/escaping packs), `dirsOf(root)` (`:71`), `spawnSync`/`http` for the real routes (`:121` "the server: GET /packs/index.json lists the packs live, GET /packs/<id>/<file> serves only what a manifest names"), `afterEach` resets globals (`:39-43`). Assertions worth copying: the skipped-reason map (`:78-86`), the exact index entry shape (`:89-92`), `compatible:false` still listed (`:94`), and a `<script>` file in a pack folder that must never be served (`:63` `packs/qab/notes.html`, `<script>alert(1)</script>`).

**Names to mirror for a new workshop module** (if you follow this house style): `WORKSHOP_DIR`, `scanWorkshop`/`loadWorkshop`, `createWorkshopRegistry` (same 5-method shape), `WORKSHOP_CONTENT_FILES`, `normalizeWorkshopManifest`, `workshopIndexEntry`, `applyWorkshop(base, packs)`, `workshopSummary(report)`, `tools/workshop.mjs list|index|check`, `PACKS_URL`-style `WORKSHOP_URL`.

## 3. ZIP CAPABILITY — **a reusable reader already exists**

- **Reader (hand-rolled, no dependency): `tools/package-update.mjs:62` `export function readZip(zipPath, onFile)`** — parses the central directory only: EOCD scan (`:69`), zip64 locator/record (`:74-83`), entry fields (`:88-99`), zip64 extra field (`:100-114`), **UTF-8 names honored; Info-ZIP Unicode Path extra field 0x7075 with CRC check otherwise** (`entryName` `:41-55`), rejects symlinks (`:117`) and encrypted entries (`:118`), methods 0 (stored) / 8 (`zlib.inflateRawSync`, `:124-126`) only, length check (`:127`) and CRC when `zlib.crc32` exists (`:128`). `SIG` constants at `:32`. No directory walk, directories skipped (`:116`), not streaming (whole entry in memory), file path input only.
- **Writer: shells out.** `tools/package.mjs:460` `zipFolder(cwd, zipPath)` → `:462` `spawnSync('zip', ['-q','-r','-X', zipPath, FOLDER])`, fallback `:464` `spawnSync('tar', ['-c','--format','zip','--options','zip:hdrcharset=UTF-8','-f', zipPath, FOLDER])`, else throws `'zipping failed: install `zip` (or a bsdtar `tar`)'` (`:465`).
- **There is NO `shared/zip.js` or zip module in this tree** (Forge has one: `app/shared/zip.js`, 397 lines). `zlib` appears elsewhere only for PNG (`tools/crop-board-atlas.mjs:36,161,209`) and woff2/Brotli (`tools/assets/woff2.mjs`). `Expand-Archive` appears only in `scripts/make-windows-bundle.mjs:554` (extracting the official Node zip via `tar`/`unzip`/`Expand-Archive` fallback chain, `:549-561`).
- **What a pack import/export CLI must add:** (a) reuse `readZip` — do not hand-roll again; (b) an **entry allowlist**: accept only `pack.json` + the content-file roles your new `PACK_TYPES` entry declares, verified with `isPackId` (`shared/packs.js:68`) and `isPackPath` (`:75`), **and refuse `*.js`/`*.mjs`/`*.html` outright** — that is the whole point of the data-only port (the existing test already asserts a `<script>` file in a pack folder is never served); (c) extraction target must be re-checked against the pack root (`server/packs.js:53` `inside()` is the precedent); (d) export needs either the `zip`/`tar` spawn or a new writer; (e) `test/update-package.test.js` has an existing readZip test pattern to copy.
- [?] `readZip` takes a *path*, so a browser-upload import would need a temp file or a Buffer variant.

## 4. PROTOCOL / ROOM SURFACE

`shared/constants.js`: `:5` `PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1`; `:8` `APP_VERSION = '0.3.0'`; `:11` `DEV_BUILD = /-dev$/`; `:13` `MAX_SEATS = 4`; `:20` `MAX_SPECTATORS = 2`; `:21` `ROOM_CODE_LEN = 4`; `:22` `NAME_MAX_LEN = 12`; `:52` `ROOM_LIST_MAX = 60`; `:385` (in `shared/protocol.js`) `CHAT_MAX_LEN = 80`.

Exact validation entries (`shared/protocol.js`):
- `:404` `'room.create': { mode: (v) => v === 'solo' || v === 'coop', difficulty: (v) => DIFFICULTIES.includes(v), mods: isModList, test: isBool, $optional: ['mods', 'test'] },`
- `:405` `'room.join': { code: (v) => isStr(v, ROOM_CODE_LEN + 2) && /^[A-Za-z0-9]+$/.test(v) },`
- `:424` `'room.quickmatch': { mode: (v) => v === 'solo' || v === 'coop', difficulty: (v) => DIFFICULTIES.includes(v) },`
- `:429` `'room.setMods': { mods: isModList },`
- `:274-275` `export const isModList = (v) => Array.isArray(v) && v.length <= MOD_IDS.length && v.every(isModId) && new Set(v).size === v.length;` — **this is the template for a new optional list field.** Comment `:269-273`: registered ids only, no duplicates, an unknown id is **refused as BAD_MSG, never silently dropped**, absent list means "the defaults" and is expressed by `$optional`.
- `RESULT_LIMITS` `:34` includes `mods: 16` (cap inside `m.result`).
- `validateC2S` `:577-590`: iterates the validator table; `:586` `if (v === undefined && optional.includes(k)) continue;`. **Unknown extra fields are ignored, not rejected** (`:575` "Extra unknown fields are ignored") — so a client sending a new field to an old server gets a silent accept, and the server simply never reads it.

**How to add a new optional field (e.g. a pack id list / digest):** mirror `mods` end-to-end — (1) `isModList`-style validator in `shared/protocol.js` (own module is possible; Forge moved its `isModList` to `shared/modIdentity.js` because **`jsconfig.json` excludes `shared/protocol.js` from the typecheck slice**); (2) add to the `room.create` object + `$optional` (`:404`); (3) thread through `server/lobby.js:733` `create(session, {mode, difficulty, mods, test})` → `:758` `room.mods = …normalizeMods(mods) : …` and `Room.state` (`lobby.js:290` `mods: [...this.mods]`); (4) a separate `room.setX` handler next to `setMods` (`lobby.js:1020`); (5) client mirror. **Also note the `room.state` field the client already aligns on:** `public/js/main.js:186` `data.setMods(room.mods);` and `:306` reset on leaving a room; `public/js/data.js:455` `setMods(value)`, `:314` `modView(name, value)`.

**Nothing compares a content/pack hash between client and server today.** No sha256/`createHash` in `shared/` or `public/` at all (the only hashing is release-packaging: `tools/package-update.mjs:152` `sha()`) [?]. The wire carries `hello.version` as an integer `(v) => isInt(v, 0, 1e6)` (`:397`) and `/healthz` carries `build: buildTag()` (`server/http/routes.js:55`) — that is the staging precedent for "a content hash in the handshake" (PACKS.md:141-142).

## 5. PACKAGING / GITIGNORE / TESTS / DOCS

- **`tools/package.mjs` includes top-level dirs only by explicit list.** `:89` `export const RUNTIME_DIRS = ['server/', 'shared/', 'data/', 'public/', 'packs/'];`, and `:147-153` `isPlayerFile(rel)`: `isRefused`/`isJunk`/`data/local-assets.json`/`packs/index.json` → false (`:149`), `NOT_TRACKED_SHIP = ['public/dev/','public/assets/','public/fonts/','public/vendor/']` → false (`:95`,`:150`), then `:151` `RUNTIME_DIRS.some(prefix) || PLAYER_TOOL_DIRS.some(prefix)` → true, plus explicit root files/docs/research/scripts/tools lists (`:74-87`). So **a new `workshop/` (or `data/workshop/`) directory does NOT ship unless added to `RUNTIME_DIRS` or a new constant**. Note: Forge itself does not ship it either (`参考对象/…Forge…/app/tools/package.mjs:88` is still `['server/','shared/','data/','public/','packs/']`), yet `workshop/README.md` is tracked and its 安装工坊包.bat creates the directory — i.e. workshop content is author/player-installed, not packaged.
- **`.gitignore` (target, 45 entries)** covers: `node_modules/`, `.npm-cache/`, `public/vendor/`, `public/assets/`, `public/fonts/`, `third-party/bundle/`, `data/local-assets.json`, `.cache/`, Python junk, **`packs/index.json`**, `test/e2e/out/`, `logs/`, `*.log`, `scripts/service.env.cmd`, `.env*`, OS/editor clutter. It does **not** ignore any `workshop/` path ([?] the sibling fork tree at the workspace root does have `workshop`/`packs` — that is a different tree, not this repo). If locally-installed packs must stay out of git you must add an entry; Forge's `.gitignore` also does not ignore `workshop/` (its `workshop/` holds a tracked README).
- **Test conventions:** `node:test` + `node:assert/strict`, `import { test } from 'node:test'`, one file per area under `test/{content,sim,match,ui,render,}/`. Hook `"test": "node --test"`. Confirmed from the user's brief: **`node --test <file>` fails here with spawn EPERM — run files as `node <testfile>`.** `test/i18n-packs.test.js` shows the end-to-end house pattern (synthetic root in `os.tmpdir()`, real HTTP route via `createStaticHandler`/`http`, `afterEach` cleanup).
- **Docs tests:**
  - `test/docs-paths.test.js:18` `DOCS = [['docs/ARCHITECTURE.md', 100], ['CONTRIBUTING.md', 15]]` — only those two files get the "every repository path it names exists" check (`:64-69`) and the "every relative link resolves" check (`:71-80`); `TOP` (`:20`) matches `server|shared|public|data|tools|test|docs|types|scripts|.github` prefixes; `EXEMPT` (`:24`) = `public/assets/`, `public/vendor/`, `data/local-assets.json`. **A new doc page is unconstrained unless you reference it from ARCHITECTURE.md / CONTRIBUTING.md, or reference a not-yet-existing file inside one of those two.** If you do mention e.g. `workshop/` or `shared/workshop.js` there before it exists, the test fails.
  - `test/docs-paths.test.js:96-126` — DESIGN index rule: every `## N.` heading must live **exactly once** in a file under `DESIGN_DIRS` (i.e. `docs/design/` or `docs/history/`), the index itself holds no section, sections must be contiguous from §0 to max, each section file's title must match `/^# DESIGN §\d+(?:, §\d+)* — .+\n\nPart of \[DESIGN\.md\]\(\.\.\/DESIGN\.md\)/` (`:103`), and the index table row must name **exactly** the sections that file holds (`:116-122`). **So: is there a docs index that must list a new design page? Yes — `docs/DESIGN.md`.** Current rows: `docs/DESIGN.md:22-41` (last is `| §29 | history/0.3.0.md | 0.3.0 — 协议源石 …|`). A new §N needs a new row **and** the file to hold exactly those sections. `:128-142` — every `DESIGN §N` cited anywhere in the repo must be a heading; ≥700 citations required.
  - `test/docs-consistency.test.js` (912 lines) pins prose↔code agreement for specific sections (§18–§21.29, data-table rows, ASSETS/DEPLOY strings). It has no generic "every doc must be listed" rule.
- Other constraints from `AGENTS.md`: `:33` "**Generated data.** Never hand-edit `data/*.json`: change `tools/build-data.mjs` and regenerate."; `:34` "**No game art in git.** `public/assets/` is ignored…"; docs rules `:38-39` "**Docs follow the code.** A rule change updates `docs/design/`, the current `docs/history/` file and the reference docs; run `node --test test/docs-consistency.test.js test/docs-paths.test.js`." Adds weight to the byte-identical requirement: `test/data.test.js:8-9` "…an offline rebuild that must reproduce data/ byte-for-byte (catches a stale data/ after a build-script change)" and `:866` the actual test (`tools/build-data.mjs --offline`) — **an overlay that rewrote `data/*.json` on disk would break this test.** `tools/build-data.mjs` `buildModMarks` is what stamps `data/backups.json.mods` (docs/MODS.md:63).

## 6. POLICY TEXT (verbatim)

`docs/PACKS.md:137-145` — "Things later types must keep (the maintainers' study of mods, 2026-10-07):"
- `:139-140` "**Server-side only for gameplay.** A data pack is installed on the server, so every player of a room uses the same content and the server can still check or recompute a battle. A player never installs gameplay content of their own."
- `:141-142` "**A content hash in the handshake** before data packs ship (`welcome`, `/healthz`, the BattleSpec), so a client and a server can tell they run the same content, and a "modded" mark in the UI and in bug reports."
- `:143-144` "**No third-party code.** The manifest and its files are data (JSON, images, audio, fonts); a pack type that runs code is a separate decision of the owner's."
- `:145` "**The golden results stay on the original content**; a pack never changes them."
Also `docs/PACKS.md:6` "In 0.2.0 one type loads, **`lang`** …; `assets` and `data` are planned."; `:112` "| `data` | planned | the server: data patches applied at start | — |"; `:114-115` "A pack of a planned type is parsed and listed as not supported ("type "assets" is planned …, not loaded by this version"); it is never served and never changes anything."; `:130-132` "A server-side type (`data`) loads once at start (`live: false`): the simulation's code and data are fixed for the life of the process, and a battle must run the same data on the server and in every browser."; `:101-104` "**What is served.** `GET /packs/<id>/<file>` answers only a file that the manifest of a loaded folder pack names under a role of its type, with that role's extension, inside the folder once links are resolved — `pack.json`, a readme, an HTML or script file in the folder are never served; a pack of a planned type serves nothing."

**Already anticipates data packs / extension points:** `docs/PACKS.md:117-121` — "**Before a `data` pack, 内容 mod** ([docs/MODS.md](MODS.md)) is the simpler mechanism the remake ships: … It is not a pack type — no manifest, no folder, the content is committed in `data/` and stamped (`data/backups.json` `mods`) — but it keeps the four rules below and its `filterData` is **where a `data` pack's payload would be applied**, so the room switch and the client filter carry over." And `docs/PACKS.md:123-135` "### Adding a pack type" (1. the `PACK_TYPES` entry + a normalizer beside `langFields` + a branch in `normalizeManifest` + a branch in `packIndexEntry`; 2. the loader reads `createPackRegistry(…).list('assets')` or `readPackIndex(json,'assets')`; 3. the checks `tools/packs.mjs check` and the tests verify).

`docs/MODS.md`:
- `:124` "**不执行第三方代码**：mod 是数据与注册表条目，不是插件脚本。"
- `:125-126` "**内容包（`packs/<id>/`）是以后的分发方式**：本机制是它的简化前置——`PACKS.md` 的 `data` 类型仍是 planned，真做那个类型时，`filterData` 就是从 pack 装载数据的位置，房间开关可以直接复用。"
- `:118` "### 有意不做的事（沿用 PACKS.md 的结论）" / `:120` "…本条机制遵守它们："
- `:122` "**游戏内容只装在服务器侧**、一场对局里人人一致；玩家自己装不了玩法内容。"
- `:123` "**黄金结果永远基于原生内容**：`filterData` 在默认下是恒等，跑测试与 golden 时数据不变。"
- `:58` "一句话：**在服务端的数据出口切断，客户端只是跟着隐藏入口**。" / `:77` "**客户端过滤是"诚实"而不是"安全"**：`/data/*.json` 是静态文件、所有房间一样…真正的边界在服务端。"
- `:82-83` "**全开时零开销**：`filterData` 在默认（全开）下返回**同一个对象**（`===`），不复制、不重建…**黄金结果不受影响**" (regression-guarded by `test/mods.test.js:135-137`, `:424-436`).

**Generated-data rule:** `AGENTS.md:33` "**Generated data.** Never hand-edit `data/*.json`: change `tools/build-data.mjs` and regenerate."

## 7. INTEGRATION SURFACE TABLE (Forge concept → this tree)

| Forge concept | This tree | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `workshop/` directory | **absent as a pack root.** Nearest: `packs/` (`server/http/config.js:62`, `server/packs.js:100`) + `public/i18n/` (`server/packs.js:78`). Its README ships: `packs/README.md` (28 lines). | config.js:62, server/packs.js:78-139 |
| `pack.json` manifest validation | **exists but type-driven, not schema-driven.** `normalizeManifest` (`shared/packs.js:151`) with `problems`/`warnings`; roles+extensions from `PACK_TYPES` (`:55-62`); no `overrides`/`content`/`author` field handling (an unknown field is a warning only when it appears under `files`, `:201`). | shared/packs.js:151-204 |
| content-file overlay (`applyWorkshop`) | **absent.** No merge seam: `server/data.js:81` `return deepFreeze(out)` straight from the parsed files. Forge's hook is at its `server/data.js:96-107`. | server/data.js:59-82 |
| `overrides` whitelist | **absent.** Nothing in this tree lets a pack replace an existing record; `normalizeManifest` has no `overrides` branch. Nearest analogue = the 内容 mod switch (`filterData` only *removes* content). | shared/packs.js:192-202; shared/mods.js:328 |
| pack identity digest (sha256) | **absent** (no hash of any pack content on disk or on the wire). Nearest: `PACK_TYPES[x].status` + `manifest.compatible` / `app` range as the only "identity" a client sees. | shared/packs.js:216-233; protocol.js:397 |
| file enumerator (`listPackFiles`) | **exists for serving only:** `scanPacks` + `manifest.files` membership, `servable(id, rel)` (`server/packs.js:238-243`) plus `inside()` realpath check (`:53`). There is no client-facing per-file inventory route. | server/packs.js:121-126, 238 |
| startup log | **exists:** `server/packs.js:217-220` `[packs] …` info line + per-skipped/per-warning warn lines, deduped by `tell()`; `server/index.js:113` `packs.refresh(true)`. `/healthz` exposes `mods` (build's mod marks), not packs. | server/packs.js:205-220; routes.js:57 |
| `/mods/catalog.json` | **absent.** Nearest: `GET /packs/index.json` → `{version, app, packs:[entry]}` (`shared/packs.js:241`; route `server/http/static.js:88`), entries carry role→URL, no per-file bytes/sha256. | static.js:86-94; shared/packs.js:214-244 |
| room-scoped pack subsets | **absent for packs; present for the 内容 mod registry:** `room.create.mods` (`protocol.js:404`), `room.setMods` (`:429`), `isModList` (`:274`), `Room.mods` (`lobby.js:209`, `:758`, `:1020-1031`), per-room data `dataFor(room)` (`lobby.js:1529` → `filterData`), broadcast in `Room.state` (`lobby.js:290`) and `lobby.stats` summaries (`protocol.js:547-548`). | protocol.js:274,404,429; lobby.js:758,1529 |
| client alignment | **partial:** mod switches only — `public/js/data.js:455` `setMods`, `:314` `modView`, `main.js:186`/`:306`; and index filtering `readPackIndex(json, type)` drops entries of other types / bad ids / non-local URLs (`shared/packs.js:256-267`). **No digest comparison, no "does the server have the same content as me" check.** | data.js:455; shared/packs.js:256-267 |
| zip import | **reader exists, exported:** `readZip(zipPath, onFile)` (`tools/package-update.mjs:62`); **no writer** (spawn only: `tools/package.mjs:460-465`); **no import/export CLI**; no `shared/zip.js`. | package-update.mjs:62; package.mjs:460 |

## Bottom line for the port

The three seams you need already have house precedents, and only one of them is a new mechanism:
1. **Overlay seam = `server/data.js:81`, before `deepFreeze`** — Forge's exact placement; keep `data/*.json` byte-identical so `test/data.test.js:866` (offline rebuild byte-for-byte) and the golden corpus keep passing. `loadData` already accepts an options object, so adding `{ workshopDir | packs, excludePacks }` is additive and `workshopDir: null` makes it a no-op for tests.
2. **Discovery/validation/manifest/error-reporting = mirror `shared/packs.js` + `server/packs.js` verbatim in shape** (`problems` vs `warnings`, `skipped[].where`, `MAX_JSON_BYTES`, `inside()`, the `tell()` log-once, the 5-method registry). Add the new type to `PACK_TYPES` (`shared/packs.js:55`) with `status:'supported', live:false` — `live:false` is already the documented meaning for a server-side type (`docs/PACKS.md:130-132`) and the `/data/*.json` route needs no change because the browser must see the merged data.
3. **The only genuinely new code** = the zip import CLI (reuse `readZip` + an allowlist that refuses `.js`/`.html`), the digest (nothing to reuse; Forge's `shared/modIdentity.js` is a self-contained pure-JS sha256 + `canonicalJson` + `modManifestDigest`/`modDigest`), and the protocol field (`room.create` `$optional` + a `room.state` field, exactly the `mods` path).

Hard blockers to respect, all quoted above: `docs/PACKS.md:143-144` "**No third-party code.** … a pack type that runs code is a separate decision of the owner's"; `docs/MODS.md:124` "**不执行第三方代码**：mod 是数据与注册表条目，不是插件脚本"; `AGENTS.md:33` never hand-edit `data/*.json`; `docs/PACKS.md:141-142` "**A content hash in the handshake**" is *anticipated but not implemented* — the `mods` list on `room.create`/`room.state` is today's only room-content agreement mechanism.

**No files were modified; no tests were run.**

## Summary of findings

**1. Data pipeline.** `server/data.js:59` `loadData(dir = DATA_DIR, { log, expected })` reads every `data/*.json` by basename (`:72`), warns on missing expected files (`:80`), and `:81 return deepFreeze(out)` — **there is no overlay/merge seam today**, and Forge's hook is exactly at that line. `getData` (`:92`) is a first-call-wins singleton; `resetData` (`:98`) only clears it for tests. Only caller in the server is `server/index.js:109`. The browser gets raw files: `server/http/static.js:61` mounts `/data/`, `files.js:186-191 cacheControlFor` makes all of `/data/` `no-cache` with size+mtime ETag, and **nothing filters them per room** (docs/MODS.md:77 says so). No hash is ever validated — the client (`public/js/data.js:169`) just fetches and JSON-parses. `server/sim/simdata.js` re-derives every record into engine defs; room filtering happens only in `lobby.dataFor(room)` (`server/lobby.js:1529` → `filterData`).

**2. Pack plumbing.** `shared/packs.js` (format, `problems` vs `warnings` at `:143-144`) + `server/packs.js` (registry: `scanPacks:69`, `createPackRegistry:199` with the 5-method surface and log-once `tell():205`) are the pattern to mirror, wired at `server/index.js:112-114` and `static.js:86-94`. The `lang` file roles + extensions at `shared/packs.js:58` are what enforce "nothing else in a pack folder is ever served".

**3. Zip.** A **reusable hand-rolled reader already exists**: `tools/package-update.mjs:62 export function readZip(zipPath, onFile)` (zip64, UTF-8/0x7075 names, stored+deflate, CRC). No writer — `tools/package.mjs:460 zipFolder` spawns `zip`/`tar`. No `shared/zip.js`. An import CLI should reuse `readZip` plus `isPackId`/`isPackPath` and refuse `.js`/`.html`.

**4. Protocol.** `room.create` at `shared/protocol.js:404` (with `$optional: ['mods','test']`), `room.join:405`, `room.quickmatch:424`, and `isModList:274` is the exact template for a new optional list field. `PROTOCOL_VERSION=1` (`shared/constants.js:5`), `MAX_SEATS=4` (`:13`). **No client/server hash comparison exists anywhere.**

**5. Packaging/docs.** `tools/package.mjs:89 RUNTIME_DIRS` is an explicit list — a new top-level dir does **not** ship by default. `docs/DESIGN.md` *is* an enforced index (`test/docs-paths.test.js:96-126`: each `## N.` exactly once, index row naming exactly those sections, ≥700 citations). Contract-doc paths are only checked in ARCHITECTURE.md/CONTRIBUTING.md.

**6. Policy.** Quoted verbatim: `docs/PACKS.md:143-144` "**No third-party code.** The manifest and its files are data…"; `docs/MODS.md:124` "**不执行第三方代码**：mod 是数据与注册表条目，不是插件脚本"; `docs/PACKS.md:117-121` already names `filterData` as "where a `data` pack's payload would be applied"; `AGENTS.md:33` never hand-edit `data/*.json` (backed by the byte-for-byte rebuild test at `test/data.test.js:866`).

## 2. DATA-ONLY pack.json FIELD SET

`PACK_FIELDS` (111-128) is a **closed set of 30 keys**; any other top-level key refuses the whole pack with `PACK_UNKNOWN_FIELD` (1438-1442). Normalized result at 1936-1969. Always-present keys (22): `id, name, version, author, license, hasAssets, description, gameVersion, api, game, layer, combat, content, overrides, voices, voiceLangs, bondIcons, itemIcons, art, support, operators, playtest`. Conditional (only when the key is literally present): `assets, client, server, routes, i18n, notices, forms, fx` (1817-1826 + 1967); `skins` only when non-empty (1961). That asymmetry is load-bearing for hashes.

Pure-data fields and their landing sites:
- `content[]` (1448-1450) — filtered to `WORKSHOP_CONTENT_FILES` (chess, units, items, enemies, stages, waves, tokens, bosses, factions, garrisons, bands, bonds, effects, choices; `config` deliberately excluded, 26-30), deduped + `.sort()`. `<f>.json` → `pack.files[f]` → `data[f]`, except `units` → `data.backups.units` (`OVERLAY_TARGET_BY_FILE` 2579).
- `overrides[]` (1453-1455) — `OVERRIDE_ENTRY_RE` filtered, deduped, sorted; used only in the merge decision (2683), never written into data.
- `voices` (1470-1510) → `assets.audio.voice` (`workshopVoiceIndex`/`mergeWorkshopVoices` 2264, 2887); URLs `WORKSHOP_MEDIA_PREFIX + packId + '/' + percent-encoded path`.
- `voiceLangs` (1516-1539) → `assets.audio.voiceLangs[lang]`, key order forced to `VOICE_LANGS`.
- `bondIcons` (1546-1560) → `assets.bonds[bondId]`.
- `itemIcons` (1568-1582) → `assets.items[itemId]`.
- `art` (1598-1625) → `assets.<table>`; flat tables (skills/profSub/bonds/bands) → `assets.skills` / `assets.prof.sub` / `assets.bonds` / `assets.bands` (`mergeWorkshopFlatArt` 3422).
- `skins[]` (1644-1706) → `assets.skins[charId]` (3194).
- `support[]` (1711-1721) → `data.support.pool[<tier>]` (2536); tier **derived** from the merged chess record.
- `operators{}` (1732-1750) → `data.backups.diy.ownedPool` + `diy.operators[charId]` (3613); name/rarity/profession/subProfessionId **derived** from the pack's own `units[charId]` (3520-3596).
- `i18n{}` (1794; parser 1075-1114) → merged `/i18n/<code>.json` bodies (2134) — not the data overlay.
- `notices{}` (1798; parser 1051-1073) → `data/notices.json` body (server/notices.js), merged at `server/index.js:154-155`.
- `forms{}` (1803-1811; parser 1116-1189) → `assets.modForms` (3274), first-party FORMS win.
- `fx.silentPhases[]` (1812; parser 1212-1243) → `assets.silentPhases` (3335).
- `playtest.directToHand[]` (1761-1778) → **no data file**; playtest server only; explicitly NOT a contribution (1844, 1855).
- Identity scalars `id,name,version,author,license,description,gameVersion,game,api,layer,combat` (1443-1456, 1888-1913, 1938-1950). `gameVersion` is an alias of `game` (`game` wins, 1908); `api` is checked against `MOD_API_VERSION` (1903).

**Excluded, with reasons:**
- `server` (780-853) — five sub-decls, each naming a `.mjs` the server `await import()`s: `preDispatch.module`+`policy`, `meta.module`(+`registers`), `modules[].entry`(+`uses`,`write`), `battle.module`, `room.module`. Hard gates: `META_NEEDS_COMBAT` 1918, `BATTLE_NEEDS_COMBAT` 1922, `MODULES_NEED_COMBAT` 1928.
- `client` (501-713) — `panels[].module` (.js loaded by the browser), `panels[].styles` (.css injected), `wraps` (engine component wrap/replace), `data`/`messages` grants. The `client.theme.vars` sub-part is pure CSS data and `requires` is a capability list, but both live under the same key — a data-only port should refuse the whole key rather than half-read it.
- `assets` (437-468) — pure **asset-container** plumbing (`container` `.spresources`, `manifest` `.json`, `serverPolicy`, `verify`). Its only data contribution is the synthetic `assets.container.sha256` row that binds container bytes into the hash.
- `routes` (985-1049) — borderline: genuinely DATA (`.json` only; `ROUTE_BAD_FILE` 1006 refuses anything else) but not merged into loaded data — it is a per-pack HTTP surface (`public`/`no-cache`/`no-store`). Keep or drop as policy; it never executes.

## 3. MERGE / OVERLAY RULES

Core rule (header 14-16): **additive by default; an official id is replaced ONLY if the pack lists `"<file>:<id>"` in `overrides`.**
- `applyWorkshop(base, packs)` 2647-2759 — pure; builds a new top object (`{...base}` 2648), never mutates input.
- **Ordering is NOT by layer.** It is `byPackId` — code-unit compare on pack id, ascending (2321), applied at **2661**: `[].filter(...).sort(byPackId)`. Smaller id wins every collision. Layers A/B/C never participate in merge order.
- Per record, in `mergeOne` (2668-2708), four ordered decisions:
  1. `contributors` hit → `PACK_ID_COLLISION` (2676-2682): another pack already contributed it; the later pack loses **even if it declared the override** (`definedBy` = holder).
  2. `exists && !declared.has(key)` → `OFFICIAL_ID_COLLISION` (2683-2689); record kept.
  3. `exists` → `unknownOverrideKeys(baseContainerFor(base,file)?.[id] ?? priorMap[id], rec)` (2693-2701) → `UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE_FIELD`.
  4. else added. Record = `exists ? mergeRecord(priorMap[id], rec) : rec` (**2707** — merged onto priorMap[id], i.e. official *or* an earlier pack's record).
- `overlayContentFile` 2596-2620: path = `OVERLAY_TARGET_BY_FILE[file] || [file]`; each path segment shallow-copied and re-attached (2618) so siblings under `backups` (`diy`, `tokens`) survive.
- `mergeRecord(base, patch)` 2417-2430 — field-level, recursive, non-mutating. Per key: `OVERRIDE_KEYED_LISTS` key + both arrays → `mergeKeyedList`; else `OVERRIDE_REPLACE_KEYS.includes(key) || Array.isArray(value)` → **replace wholesale**; else recurse for plain objects; else take the value. `OVERRIDE_REPLACE_KEYS` = `['skill','skills','trait','traitBase','traitOverride','modules','rangeGrid','attackRangeGrid','assets','diy','bonds']` (2378-2381).
- `OVERRIDE_KEYED_LISTS` = `{talents:'index', talentsBase:'index', talentChanges:'talentIndex'}` (2407). `mergeKeyedList` 2447-2470: pairs by integer key, base order preserved, unmatched patch entries appended; **a duplicate key on either side falls back to wholesale replace** (2449-2458) — `talentChanges` uses `-1` for hidden module talents.
- `unknownOverrideKeys` 2479-2482: `Object.keys(patch).filter((k) => !Object.hasOwn(base, k))`.
- `normalizeContentFile(file, json)` 2012-2033: plain object (`BAD_CONTENT`); key matches `RECORD_ID_RE` (169) `^[A-Za-z0-9_\-.:]{1,64}$` (`BAD_RECORD_ID`); value plain object (`BAD_RECORD`); `ID_FIELD_BY_FILE` (33-37: chess→chessId, units→charId, items→id, enemies→key, stages→stageId, waves→templateId, tokens→tokenId, bosses→bossId, factions→factionId, garrisons→garrisonId, bands→bandId, bonds→bondId, effects→effectId, choices→id) when present must equal the key (`ID_MISMATCH` 2020-2022); `units` must pass all six `UNIT_REQUIRED_FIELDS` 49-86 (`UNIT_MISSING_CHAR_ID`, `UNIT_MISSING_NAME`, `UNIT_BAD_RARITY`, `UNIT_MISSING_PROFESSION`, `UNIT_MISSING_SUB_PROFESSION`, `UNIT_BAD_FORMS`); zero records → `EMPTY_CONTENT` (2031).
- Dedup/order everywhere: `[...new Set(...)].sort()` for `content`/`overrides`; `stableStringList` 208; `orderedOperators` sorted by charId 1750; `directToHand.sort()` 1777; `parseStringList` sorts 1261; voice lists `[...new Set(list)].sort()` 1502; `orderedLangLines` forced to `VOICE_LANGS` 1539.
- `EMPTY_PACK` 1847-1862, checked after ALL contributions parse. Contribution = any `content`, `voices`, `voiceLangs`, `bondIcons`, `itemIcons`, `art`, `skins`, `operators`, or any declared key that `contributesDecl` counts — `fx.silentPhases: []` deliberately does **not** count (1840). `support` and `playtest` are named in the message as non-contributions.
- Post-record merges, in this exact order (2713-2738): `linkWorkshopStages` → `mergeWorkshopVoices` → `mergeWorkshopBondIcons` → `mergeWorkshopItemIcons` → `mergeWorkshopArt` → `mergeWorkshopFlatArt` → `mergeWorkshopSkins` → `mergeWorkshopForms` → `mergeWorkshopFx` → `mergeWorkshopSupport` → `mergeWorkshopOperators`.
- `linkWorkshopStages` 3658-3678: appends each newly added stage id to the `config.modes[m].stages` lists its own `modes[]` names; nothing else in `config` is ever written.
- `report` (2649) = `{packs:[{id,name,files:{<file>:{added,overridden}}}], added, overridden, errors, looks, overlaps, strippedPool, poolStripped, support, operators, voiceLangs, bondIcons, itemIcons, art, skinsByPack, flatArt, linkedStages, supportOff}`; `added`/`overridden` lists sorted at 2756-2757.

**FULL ERROR/WARNING CODE LIST**

## 4. IDENTITY — byte-compatible recipe

Order of operations in `identifyPack` (825-987), digest at the end by `modManifestDigest`:
1. `manifest = []`, `seen = Set`; `add(rel,hash)` = **first-wins** dedupe by path (828-830).
2. `addText('pack.json', canonicalJson(<normalized manifest>))` — **834**. The file's *bytes* are not hashed; the normalized object is. It includes `api`/`layer`/`combat` as declared (not derived) and `hasAssets` (from `fs.existsSync(packDir+'/assets')` — so an empty `assets/` dir changes the hash).
3. For each `[file, records]` of `pack.files`: `addText('<file>.json', canonicalJson(records))` — 836 (normalized records, not disk bytes).
4. For each `rel` of `listPackFiles(packDir)` not already seen: `addBytes(rel, fs.readFileSync(...))` — 846-851. **The whole pack directory**: recursive, every regular file, sorted by code-unit path, symlinks neither followed nor listed, no name/dir exclusions (so `README.md`, `kits/**`, `assets/**`, `*.bak` all count). Unreadable → skipped silently (850).
5. `kits` count 852-863: `readdirSync(packDir+'/kits').sort()`, names ending `.js`, `statSync().isFile()`, not starting with `_` — used only to derive `layer`/`combat`; the bytes already came from step 4.
6/6b/7/8 (864-984): extra `addBytes` passes for declared paths already covered by step 4 — `client.panels[].module` 868-878, `client.panels[].styles[]` 882-890, `server.meta.module` 895-902, `server.battle.module` 906-913, `server.modules[].entry` 917-931, `server.room.module` 924-937, `i18n`+`notices` values (`declaredTextFiles` 972-984). Each guarded by `manifest.some(...)` and `abs.startsWith(packDir + path.sep)`. **All no-ops for a data-only pack.**
   **Step 6, the synthetic row (959-967):** `if (typeof assetsDigest === 'string' && assetsDigest) add(ASSETS_DIGEST_PATH, assetsDigest)` with `ASSETS_DIGEST_PATH = 'assets.container.sha256'` (server/packFiles.js:28) and `assetsDigest` = sha256 of the container **bytes**, verified against the `<container>.sha256` sidecar at load (`assetsIssues` 342-422, `readSha256Sidecar` 312). No bytes on disk; the download route serves the 64-char digest text (`containerDigestBytes` packFiles.js:77). A real file of that name wins via step 4's `seen` dedupe.
9. `manifest.sort((a,b) => a.path < b.path ? -1 : a.path > b.path ? 1 : 0)` (985) — UTF-16 code-unit order, same comparator as `packFiles.byPath` / `modCatalog.byPath`.
10. `hash = modManifestDigest(manifest)` (986) = `sha256Hex(canonicalJson(sorted [[path,hash],…]))`.

`canonicalJson(value)` (modIdentity.js:168-174): scalars → `JSON.stringify(value) ?? 'null'`; arrays → `[a,b,c]` (comma, no spaces); objects → `{` + keys filtered by `rec[k] !== undefined` + `.sort()` (default UTF-16 lexicographic) + `"key":value` joined with `,` + `}`. No whitespace; key order irrelevant.
`modManifestDigest(manifest)` (182-188): filter entries with string `path` + string `hash` → `[path, hash]` pairs → sort by path (same comparator) → `sha256Hex(canonicalJson(pairs))`, i.e. `[["a.js","deadbeef…"],["b.js","…"]]`.
`sha256Hex` (122-160): hand-written sha256 over hand-rolled UTF-8 (`utf8Bytes` 102-115, to avoid `TextEncoder`), lowercase hex, `padStart(8,'0')` per word. Node's `createHash('sha256')` yields the same string (modCatalog.js:226-228 relies on that).
Subtleties: (a) steps 2/3 hash canonical JSON, step 4 raw bytes — swapping them changes every hash; (b) `undefined` values vanish from canonical objects but explicit `null` does not — `normalizePackManifest` writes `api: null`, `layer: null`, `combat: null` explicitly (1947-1950) while *omitting* `assets/client/server/routes/i18n/notices/forms/fx/skins` when undeclared (1817-1826, 1961, 1967), and that asymmetry is exactly what keeps old packs' hashes stable; (c) `content`/`overrides`/`support`/`directToHand`/operator key order are all sorted before hashing; (d) path strings use `/` with no leading `./` (packFiles.js:60).
Room/mod-set digest: `modDigest(list)` (196-202) over sorted `[id, hash]` pairs → 64-hex; `modSetOf` (205-210) → `{digest, packs}` sorted by id, or `null` for an empty set.

## 5. LOADER DATA PATH

`server/data.js:loadData` (67-109): read `data/*.json` (79-86) → warn on missing expected (87-88) → **if `workshopDir !== null`** (96): `loadWorkshop(workshopDir, {log})` (97) → optional `excludePacks` filter (98) → `applyWorkshop(out, packs)` (100) → copy merged top-level keys back (101) → `log.info?.('[workshop] applied N pack(s): ' + workshopSummary(report))` (102) → one `log.warn` per `report.errors` as `[workshop] <pack>: <reason>` (103) → one per `report.looks` (105) → `return deepFreeze(out)` (108). The overlay runs **before** `deepFreeze`, so every consumer sees one ordinary frozen merged object.

`server/workshop.js:loadWorkshop(dir = WORKSHOP_DIR, {log, c2s})` 87-223, in order:
1. `readdirSync(dir, {withFileTypes:true})`, directories not starting with `.`, names `.sort()` (94-97). A throw (no `workshop/`) → `{dir, present:false, packs:[], errors:[]}` — **the normal plain-install case** (98-101).
2. Per pack dir: parse `pack.json` (105-110). `ENOENT` → `pack.json is missing`; else `pack.json is unreadable: <msg>`. **`continue` = the pack is skipped**, never thrown.
3. `hasAssets = fs.existsSync(packDir + '/assets')` (112) → `normalizePackManifest(rawManifest, name, {hasAssets})` (113-117).
4. For each `file` of `manifest.pack.content`: parse + `normalizeContentFile` → `files[file] = content.records` (120-137). A per-file failure pushes an error and `continue`s the **file** loop; the rest of the pack still loads.
5. "Does this pack exist at all?" gate (151-163): loaded iff any of `files`, `voices`, `voiceLangs`, `bondIcons`, `itemIcons`, `art`, `skins.length`, `operators`, or any of `assets`/`client`/`server`/`routes`/`i18n`/`notices`.
6. `playtestUnknownIds(...)` → `PLAYTEST_UNKNOWN_CHESS` → **whole-pack skip** (166-173).
7. Declared-payload gates; the FIRST issue from any refuses the **whole** pack (178-209): `panelModuleIssues` 178, `assetsIssues` 189, `preDispatchIssues` 190, `i18nIssues` 193, `metaIssues` 196, `battleIssues` 198, `serverModuleIssues` 200, `roomIssues` 202, `noticesIssues` 204; combined at 205, first code+reason logged at 207.
8. Success → `packs.push({ ...manifest.pack, dir: packDir, files, ...identifyPack(packDir, manifest.pack, files, { assetsDigest: assetIssues.digest }), ...(assetIssues.digest ? { assetsDigest } : {}) })` (214-218). Note the spread order: `identifyPack`'s **derived** `layer`/`combat`/`api`/`game`/`hash`/`manifest` overwrite the declared ones on the entry.
9. After the loop, every error is logged as `[workshop] <pack>: <reason>` (221). Broken packs never throw; they are absent from `packs` and present in `errors`.

**Order:** `loadWorkshop` sorts directory names (`String` default), but `applyWorkshop` re-sorts by `byPackId` (2661), so merge order is pack-id code-unit ascending, independent of `readdir` order. There is no layer A/B/C ordering anywhere.

`server/http/workshop.js:buildWorkshopDataFiles(data, workshop)` 76-85 builds merged `/data/*.json` bodies for exactly `workshopTouchedFiles(workshop)` (server/workshop.js:999-1041): union of `Object.keys(p.files)` with `units` remapped to `backups` (1006), plus `assets` for voices/voiceLangs/bondIcons/itemIcons/art/skins/forms/fx (1012-1033), plus `support` (1035) and `backups` for operators (1038). `static.js:255-265` serves those ahead of the on-disk mount with `Cache-Control: no-cache` and `ETag: "ws-<sha1(body)[:20]>"` — a cache validator, not an integrity gate.

## 6. COUPLING — what the DATA path needs, and where it touches code machinery

Cannot cut: `shared/workshop.js`, `shared/modIdentity.js`, `server/packFiles.js`, `server/workshop.js:loadWorkshop`+`identifyPack`+`assetsIssues`(+`sha256FileSync`/`readSha256Sidecar`) if you keep `assets`, `server/data.js:loadData`, and `server/http/workshop.js:buildWorkshopDataFiles`+`workshopTouchedFiles` if browsers get merged data.
Transitive import edges of `shared/workshop.js` (171-182), dragged in whether used or not: `shared/constants.js`, `shared/i18nPacks.js`, `shared/support.js`, `shared/packs.js`, `shared/modIdentity.js`, `shared/diy.js` (`requiredUnitForms`, used only at 3563), `shared/packForms.js`, `shared/formNames.js`, and **`shared/protocol.js`** for `C2S` (182, used at 423 `PRE_DISPATCH_INTERCEPTS = Object.keys(C2S).sort()`). `shared/protocol.js` is 56 KB and excluded from the repo's typecheck slice (modIdentity.js header 3-6). Delete the `C2S` import + `PRE_DISPATCH_INTERCEPTS` together with the `server` field.
Direct answers:
- **Does `identifyPack` hash kit files?** YES, unconditionally and indirectly: step 4 hashes every regular file under the pack dir, including `kits/**` and helpers. It also *counts* top-level non-`_` `kits/*.js` (854-862) purely to derive `layer`/`combat` (952/958). So you cannot drop `kits/` and stay byte-compatible — but you never need to **execute** a kit to hash it, and you do **not** need `shared/kitImports.js` for identity.
- **Does `normalizePackManifest` validate `server.*`?** YES — all five members (780-983), including the `combat: true` hard gates (1918-1930). Removing `server` from `PACK_FIELDS` makes every `server`-declaring pack fail `PACK_UNKNOWN_FIELD` — a deliberate, visible change. A softer option: keep parsing it for shape (hashes unchanged) while never loading the modules.
- **Does the loader need `kitImports.js` to compute identity?** NO. It is imported at `server/workshop.js:28` and used only in `relativeModuleUrl` (1055-1060, via `packRelativePath`) and `loadWorkshopKits` (1211-1223) — the **executed-code** path. `identifyPack` and the data half of `loadWorkshop` never call it.
- **Does the loader need the `client.panels` machinery for identity?** Only for hashing declared module/style paths, which step 4 already covers; `panelModuleIssues` (240) is a disk-existence gate for the executed C layer.
- Other couplings: `server/data.js` imports `./workshop.js`, which imports `shared/kitImports.js`, `../shared/protocol.js`, `./notices.js` — so keeping `server/data.js` as-is drags the entire code-loading module into the graph even if `loadWorkshopKits` is never called. Split `server/workshop.js` into a loader module (no `await import`) and a code-loading module.
- `shared/modSurface.js` is dead here (no runtime importer) — droppable.
- `server/modCatalog.js` re-runs `normalizePackManifest` (107-116) and reads every listed file; data-only, but required only for the client download path.

## 7. CLIENT REQUIREMENTS FOR A LOCAL-ONLY DATA PACK

**If the server already applies the overlay and serves merged `/data/*.json`, the client needs NO change at all.** `public/js/data.js` (`createDataStore`, 130+; default base `'/data/'`, 161, 407) fetches the JSON and parses it; **nothing on the client validates `/data/*.json` against any digest or pack hash**. `server/http/static.js:255-265` sends the merged body with `no-cache` + `ETag: "ws-<sha1(body)[:20]>"`, which is purely a cache validator.
What actually requires the mod store:
1. **Room-declared mod sets.** The only digest gate in the system is the room handshake and it lives entirely in the client mod store: `welcome.mods`/`BattleSpec.mods` carry `{digest, packs}` (`modSetOf`, modIdentity.js:205-210); `public/js/mods/align.js:planAlignment` (94-114) rebuilds the digest from LOCAL bytes and compares `held !== mods.digest` (113); `align.js:roomDataBase` (47-51) switches the sim data face to `/room-data/<digest>/` only when the room digest differs from the process digest. `public/js/battle/runner.js:1037-1043` (`dataBaseForBattle` → `roomDataBase(spec.mods, currentModSet())`) is the only consumer.
2. **What breaks if you delete `modCatalog.js` / `server/http/mods.js` / `public/js/mods/{sync,store,align}.js`:** static imports break the bundle, not the data path — `public/js/battle/runner.js:76` imports `roomDataBase` from `../mods/align.js`, and `public/js/screens/room.js:32` (+ `:345`, `:367`) imports `loadCatalog/planAlignment/alignRoom/missingPackIds/storeFor`. Those imports and their UI must go too; `dataBaseForBattle` then degrades to a constant `'/data/'`, and you must stop emitting `welcome.mods` (`server/index.js:172`, `:258`). Server counterparts to drop: `createModsRoute`/`buildModCatalog`/`serveMods` (`server/index.js:232`, `:265`, `:272`), `roomAssets` (`server/index.js:183-197`, `server/roomAssets.js`), and the `/room-data/` route (`static.js:233-250`).
3. **What you MUST keep even with no client mod code:** `workshopTouchedFiles` → `buildWorkshopDataFiles` → the `/data/*.json` merged branch (`static.js:255-265`). Miss it and the server has overlay data while the browser fetches the on-disk files — a silent server/client split (called out in `shared/workshop.js` header 5-12 and `server/http/workshop.js:67-75`). No assets container, no `/mods/*`, no `/workshop-*`, no kits, no panels, no `room-data` are needed.
4. If you keep the room-digest feature without the download machinery: the digest must still be computable (`modDigest`/`modSetOf`) and the client still needs `modIdentity.js` — browser-safe by design (hand-rolled sha256 + `canonicalJson`, no `node:crypto`, no `TextEncoder`; modIdentity.js:78-160). `localPackHash` (sync.js:264) uses `crypto.subtle` with a pure-JS fallback (sync.js:54-63). Dropping only `sync.js` while keeping `align.js` is impossible: `align.js:16` imports `CATALOG_URL, localPackHash, missing, sync`.

All line ranges and code lists above come from direct reads of the ranges I opened plus exhaustive greps of export/function/code-string sites.

8	
9	## 0. 结论
10	
11	1. **值得做，但不该照搬 Forge 的 `workshop/` 系统。** Forge 的工坊是一整套平行设施（独立目录、独立注册表、`modCatalog`、客户端 `sync/align`、房间按摘要物化 `roomAssets`）。我们树里**已经有等价的两块地基**：
12	   - 内容包机制（`packs/` + `shared/packs.js` 的类型表 + `server/packs.js` 注册表 + `GET /packs/index.json` / `GET /packs/<id>/<file>` 白名单服务）；
13	   - 房间级内容开关 + 服务端权威数据过滤（`shared/mods.js` `filterData` + `server/lobby.js:1529 dataFor(room)` + 客户端 `public/js/data.js:308-323`）。
14	   而我们自己的文档已经把路铺好了：`docs/PACKS.md:112` 写着 `data` 类型（planned，服务端启动时应用的数据补丁），`:117-121` 写着「做 data 包之前，内容 mod 是简化前置……**它的 `filterData` 就是 data 包载荷会被应用的位置**，房间开关与客户端过滤可以直接沿用」。
15	2. **所以移植物是 Forge 的「数据规则」，不是它的设施**：内容文件规范化 + 只增不改 + `overrides` 显式白名单 + 顶层键闭集拒绝 + 记录自检 + 摘要身份。执行面（`kits/*.js`、`server.meta/modules/battle/room`、`client.panels`、`routes`、`assets` 容器、`voices/skins/forms/fx`）**一格都不搬**。
16	3. **必须在 P1 就解决的一致性硬点**：我们的浏览器**直接静态取 `data/*.json`**（`server/http/static.js:61` 挂 `/data/`；`tools/package.mjs:250` 把 `/data/` 映射到 `data/`），而服务端是在内存里叠加/过滤（`server/data.js:81` 冻结、`server/lobby.js:1529` 按房间过滤）。**服务端内存里的包内容不会自动到达浏览器**，所以「只做服务端」不成立。Forge 的解法最省事，照抄即可：**把合并后的正文由服务端在内存里产出、并先于磁盘挂载地应答**（`server/http/workshop.js:buildWorkshopDataFiles` + `server/workshop.js:999 workshopTouchedFiles`，`static.js:255-265` 带 `Cache-Control: no-cache` 与 `ETag: "ws-<sha1(body)[:20]>"`）⇒ **浏览器零改动**。注意 ETag 只是缓存校验、**不是**完整性判据，所以 `docs/PACKS.md:141-142` 要求的内容哈希握手仍然要做。
17	4. 规模：P1 约 **1,300–1,900 行**改动/新增（其中共享规则模块 600–900 行，其余是类型表、注册表、数据管线 seam、客户端合并、工具、测试、文档），P2（房间子集 + 准备前对齐）再加 **400–800 行**。不引入任何新依赖。
18

51	| `/data/*.json` 是静态的 | `server/http/static.js:61` 挂 `/data/`；`server/http/files.js:186-191` 对 `/data/` 一律 `no-cache` + size/mtime ETag；`docs/MODS.md:77` 明说「`/data/*.json` 是静态文件、所有房间一样」 | ⇒ 合并正文必须在**服务端这一层替换**（Forge 的做法），否则浏览器永远只看到磁盘原文 |

252	
253	## 9. 交叉复核补充（Forge 侧 + 我们这侧逐行核对结果）
254	
255	这一节是把两侧源码逐行核对后补进来的实现细节，写代码时按这里抄，不要凭印象。
256	
257	### 9.1 Forge 的「数据层」其实不含任何执行
258	
259	- `shared/workshop.js`（3,744 行）**没有任何执行**：它只做清单/内容文件的形状校验与叠加合并，连 `import()` 都没有；`kits` / `client.panels` / `server.*` 只是被**校验声明形状**（`parseClientDecl:501`、`parseServerDecl:780`）。
260	- 执行发生在 `server/workshop.js`：`loadWorkshopKits:1105-1283`（`await import()` 在 `:1222`）、`loadWorkshopHooks:1285-1370`（`:1321`）、`dropUnavailablePreDispatchPacks:1372`。⇒ 把**共享层的数据规则整套搬走、把装载层的执行部分整段丢掉**，在 Forge 里也是一条干净的切线。
261	- `shared/modSurface.js`（424 行）在发行包里是**死代码**（只有未随包发布的 `test/modSurface.test.js` 引用它）⇒ 我们完全不需要它。`shared/kitImports.js`（407 行）只被执行的 kit 路径用（`relativeModuleUrl:1055`、`loadWorkshopKits`），**身份摘要不需要它**。
262	- `shared/workshop.js` 的传递依赖里有一条要小心：它 import `shared/protocol.js` 只为 `C2S`（`:182`，用于 `PRE_DISPATCH_INTERCEPTS` `:423`）。我们丢掉 `server` 字段的同时把这两处一起删，就不会把 56 KB 的 protocol 拖进新模块。
263	
264	### 9.2 合并顺序与记录级合并（必须照抄的语义）
265	
266	- 叠加顺序**不是** layer A/B/C，而是 `byPackId`：按包 id 的 UTF-16 码元升序（`shared/workshop.js:2321`，用于 `:2661`）。**id 小的赢**每一次冲突。
267	- 记录级四条判定（`mergeOne` 回调 `:2668-2708`）依次是：① 别的包已贡献过同一 `<file>:<id>` ⇒ `PACK_ID_COLLISION`（后来的输，**即使它声明了覆盖**）；② 官方已有该 id 且本包没在 `overrides` 里点名 ⇒ `OFFICIAL_ID_COLLISION`（保留官方）；③ 声明了覆盖但补丁里有官方记录不存在的键 ⇒ `UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE_FIELD`（闭世界）；④ 否则新增/覆盖。覆盖时是 `mergeRecord(priorMap[id], rec)`（`:2707`）——叠加在「官方或更早那个包的记录」上，不是叠加在 `base` 上。

评估做完了，写在 [04-文档/工坊包数据层移植评估.md](04-文档/工坊包数据层移植评估.md)（315 行，含两个只读子代理的逐行复核结果）。

## 一句话结论

**搬 Forge 的「数据规则」，不搬它的「设施」。** 我们树里已经有两块等价地基，而且我们自己的文档早就把路铺好了：[docs/PACKS.md](Stronghold-Protocol-repo/docs/PACKS.md) 第 112 行写着 `data` 类型（planned，服务端启动时应用的数据补丁），第 117-121 行直接点名「`filterData` 就是 data 包载荷会被应用的位置，房间开关与客户端过滤可以直接沿用」。

## 关键发现（都核到行）

| 事实 | 位置 |
|---|---|
| 叠加 seam 就是**一行**：`server/data.js:81 deepFreeze(out)` 之前；`loadData` 已收 options，加 `{packsDir}` 是纯增量，给 `null` 即今天的行为 | `server/data.js:59-82` |
| `data` 类型的插槽已经存在：`PACK_TYPES.data = { status:'planned' }`，只需变 `supported` + 补 `files` 角色；注册表/索引/工具/白名单服务全都现成 | `shared/packs.js:55-62`、`server/packs.js`、`server/http/static.js:85-93` |
| `shared/workshop.js`（Forge 那 3,744 行）**自己不含任何执行**——执行全在 `server/workshop.js` 的 `loadWorkshopKits`/`loadWorkshopHooks`（`await import()` 在 `:1222`/`:1321`）。所以「只搬数据规则」在 Forge 里就是一条干净的切线 | §9.1 |
| 合并语义（要照抄的）：叠加顺序按**包 id 码元升序**（不是 layer），**id 小的赢**；官方 id 只有在 `overrides` 里点名才可替换；记录级是字段递归合并 + `OVERRIDE_REPLACE_KEYS` 整段替换 | §9.2 |
| zip 能力我们**已有**：纯 Node 的 `readZip`（zip64/deflate/UTF-8/0x7075/CRC/拒软链）在 `tools/package-update.mjs:62`，写入器 `tools/package.mjs:460`（spawn `zip`/`tar`） | §9.7 |
| 今天两端**没有任何哈希比对**（`/data/*.json` 只有 size+mtime ETag）——而 [docs/PACKS.md](Stronghold-Protocol-repo/docs/PACKS.md) 第 141-142 行要求 data 包上线前必须有内容哈希进握手，所以这是要从零加的一块 | §9.6 |

## 我在评估过程中改掉的一个判断

我一开始的方案是「让浏览器自己拉包、用同一份 shared 代码本地合并」。核对后发现 **Forge 有更省事的解法**：服务端把合并后的正文直接在内存里产出、**先于磁盘挂载地应答 `/data/<f>.json`**（`server/http/workshop.js:buildWorkshopDataFiles` + `workshopTouchedFiles`，带 `no-cache` + `ETag: "ws-<sha1(body)[:20]>"`）。照抄这条 ⇒ **浏览器零改动**，也不存在「两端实现漂移」的可能。评估文档已按这个方案改写（§0.3、§3.1、§3.2、§4、§5、§7 全部对齐）。

## 规模与分期

- **P1**（data 类型落地 + 两端同源 + 摘要进握手）：≈**1,300–1,900 行**，无新依赖。核心是新 `shared/dataPacks.js`（Forge 数据规则的裁剪版，600–900 行），其余是类型表、注册表装载、数据 seam、合并正文路由、工具、测试、文档。
- **P2**（房间子集 + 准备前对齐）：再 **400–800 行**，复用现有 mod 开关链（`room.create.mods` → `isModList` → `filterData` → `Room.state`）。
- **P3**（zip 导入导出）：读能力已有，只差一步「解到临时目录 → 校验清单 → 搬进 `packs/<id>/`」；建议先只支持目录形态。
- **永不搬**：`kits/*.js`、`server.meta/modules/battle/room`、`client.panels`、`routes`、`assets` 容器、语音/时装/形态——理由就写在 [docs/MODS.md:124](Stronghold-Protocol-repo/docs/MODS.md)「不执行第三方代码」与 [docs/PACKS.md:143-144](Stronghold-Protocol-repo/docs/PACKS.md)「No third-party code」。

## 需要你拍板的四件事（文档 §6 有详述）

1. 第三方包要不要提交进 git（我们的 `packs/` 是「提交才随发布包走」，Forge 是纯本机目录）；
2. 内容哈希握手是否 P1 就做（我们自己的文档要求「上线前」，我建议做）；
3. `assets` 类型（立绘/图标）要不要跟着做——不做的话 data 包只能带规则，新干员没有立绘；
4. 房间级子集（P2）是否必要——只要「服务器整体加装」的话 P1 就够。

要我直接开 P1 吗？可以先把 `shared/dataPacks.js` + `shared/packs.js` 的类型条目 + `server/data.js` 的 seam 和对应测试做出来（不碰协议与房间层），跑通「装一个 fixture 包 → 服务端内存叠加 → 浏览器拿到合并正文」这条端到端。
